## Active development policy — owner-directed acceleration (2026-09-10)

The owner directs necessary policy changes going forward so this integration is quick and efficient. This prospective section governs development of this single combined app and supersedes only the conflicting serial-development, single-correction, per-card reexecution and whole-checkout-freeze process clauses identified here, whether below or inherited. It does not amend frozen product contracts, historical acceptance, the retained final integration requirements, or owner-only release boundaries. The earlier diagnosis, path lists, runs and reviews below remain immutable chronology, not repeated work orders.

### Work in integration batches, not miniature card cycles

- Organize remaining work by dependency: populated-store migration/recovery; one production lifecycle and real feature routes; Phase 10 adoption on those routes; final combined verification. Independent work may proceed concurrently. A provisional card does not require a separate authority commit, native run or ceremony merely because its number precedes another card. Preserve every card's requirement/disposition and dependency semantics in the final coverage reconciliation; do not manufacture individual accepted receipts.
- Before a workstream's first edit, record its concrete requirement, exact fully expanded existing file paths, source evidence, preserved invariants, owner, shared-contract boundaries, paired tests, expected evidence/selectors and dependency/predecessor checkpoint in this task. Several compatible causal corrections may share that finite workset and one reviewed checkpoint. A causal neighboring declaration, caller or paired test requires a short source-grounded explicit addition before its edit, not a new owner question or a standalone authority commit. New production files/resources/project changes still require explicit causal scope rather than this neighbor rule. This is not blanket permission over directories, unrelated features or unowned files. Commit the scope record with the implementation it governs.
- Reuse existing implementers with disjoint file ownership and one independent integration reviewer. The root is the single committer, preserving direct-child linear correction history after the already recorded source merge. One owner integrates shared contracts; dependent lanes may use an agreed interface but cannot invent stubs, a parallel architecture or a second writer. Resolve consequential interface questions before parallel implementation. Review actual combined changes incrementally, retaining prior findings until their source or dependency contract changes.
- Use one concise checkpoint per meaningful batch: requirement/path map, actual changes, review, checks, exact native question and remaining gaps. Keep detailed compiler output and file checksums in the original evidence, not repeatedly copied into authority prose. Append HANDOFF at meaningful verification/integration boundaries; do not create an implementation/authority/handoff commit triplet for each small repair.

### Keep useful work moving while CI runs

- A dispatched commit, its inputs and original artifacts are immutable; the local working tree is not globally frozen during a development diagnostic. After confirming the run's exact head/ref and checkout identity, prepare and review a descendant while that original run completes. Non-force pushing a descendant is permitted only after the original checkout/source binding is confirmed and no consumer resolves mutable branch bytes. Original results remain evidence for their own head, never for the descendant.
- Do not queue speculative replacement runs, duplicate a question/shard, cancel a run, rerun a failed run ID, or dispatch a known unchanged deterministic failure. The descendant diagnostic waits for its predecessor diagnostic to be terminal and its original evidence to be audited once; do not infer a failure from unfinished output. Independent source-proven corrections and implementation do not wait for an unrelated run to finish. If a run is already active, use its result instead of launching the same question again.
- Before each batch mutation, commit/push or dispatch, refresh the facts that can change: ownership/dirty state, relevant refs and, for dispatch, live capacity and exact inputs. Reuse verified frozen authority readings, source inventories, review results and deterministic-check results only while their actual inputs and commands remain unchanged. A changed relevant digest invalidates that result. Do not rerun unchanged broad scans or re-read the entire historical program on every small edit.

### Right-size development tests; preserve final coverage

- Each development dispatch answers one stated question using the smallest useful source-defined test set and the existing supported N8, P12 or F25 tier. The development selector may focus on the changed dependency closure rather than accumulating all earlier methods into every run. No test is deleted, disabled, weakened or marked passing because it was not selected. The existing 33-method set at `e68a1e5f78de29edf2dd381f93875eff28ea31d0` is the minimum retained regression seed, augmented by every later accepted causal regression and the complete retained dependency/journey inventory; it is not a final coverage ceiling. Causally corrected methods carry the same semantic obligations.
- Run supported deterministic source checks before hosted work. Use compiler diagnostics to repair compatible errors as a reviewed batch. A source-proven blocker in the selected question is fixed first; there is no run merely to rediscover it. Storage, writer, recovery, report/media, entitlement and data-rights changes require their affected native dependency-closure tests, not only a fresh empty-store smoke. The complete required compatibility corpus and retained essential journeys remain due on the final candidate.
- Keep the already approved smallest current-runtime affected-state visual policy below. Derive states from actual wired screens and risk; group continuous journeys and shared-layout representatives without dropping distinct obligations. No automatic historical 67-state/profile replay, minimum-runtime smoke or deferred physical work. New visual evidence still needs genuine human review.
- One collector owns each original run. Verify its complete original integrity once, preserve its failures/warnings and reuse the verified local hashes. Recheck live ownership/expiry or newly changed evidence when relevant; do not duplicate downloads and unchanged full audits. Compilation, selected-unit success, UI progress, visual review and formal acceptance remain distinct claims.

### Native route and final freeze

The owner subsequently approved: "Great Lets do that and include that in now before major CI work so we can use it from the begining as well." This accepts the proposed dual GitHub/Bitrise acceptance policy prospectively for this integration. Reuse the existing named GitHub dispatcher/reusable worker and existing Bitrise Build Hub/cache connection. Both providers may supply their own exact-head native acceptance evidence after the relevant route is qualified; historic development evidence is never promoted. GitHub remains the final exact-main verification route. This supersedes the earlier GitHub-only integration restriction, not historical S10 contracts, product requirements, timeouts or native test meaning.

Qualify the ordinary current-runtime route with a controlled same-head comparison: same checked-in project/scheme/configuration, Xcode26.6/17F113 and iPhone17/iOS26.2/23C54, selected native methods, clean source binding and complete original artifacts. Preserve provider-specific resolved Xcode paths and the existing pinned Bitrise ARM64/macOS26.6.1 runner, rather than pretending the host images are identical. Compilation/cache, selected-unit and UI route qualification are distinct: a compiler diagnostic may use the new route immediately after source review, but does not qualify unit/UI execution; initial successful paired native methods qualify their actual execution kind, and the first UI comparison must exercise the relevant continuous journey. Repeat provider-equivalence qualification when its causal toolchain/runtime/cache/native-command/evidence protocol changes, not automatically for every ordinary app edit. Every later run still independently binds and tests its own exact source head. A cached compilation output never substitutes for executing selected tests.

Prefer Bitrise for cache-benefiting iterative builds and eligible coverage, GitHub for complementary coverage and independent final verification. Use up to five GitHub and three Bitrise useful dependency-ready jobs only where source-defined selections support distinct work and fresh repository capacity permits. The one explicit provider-equivalence comparison is a different question from routine coverage; do not duplicate all suites on both providers, queue speculative replacements or fill idle runners with redundant work. First reuse the ordinary one-job selected-test route; a later split needs its concrete checked-in selection/dependency map, not arbitrary selector overrides or another scheduler. Existing shared-build/S10.4 controller routes are not automatically generalized or qualified by this approval. No GetMac/Warp, minimum runtime, release action, new service/secret/account or timeout increase is authorized.

Freeze final candidate bytes only once production adoption and required source corrections are complete. Then collect the complete retained same-head functional/compatibility and minimal affected-state visual evidence, independent integration review and genuine human review. A correction makes a new exact candidate and invalidates affected final evidence; historical runs are never promoted across heads. Advance `main` only by the existing verified non-force fast-forward to the unchanged qualified candidate, followed by required exact-main verification. Main stays at accepted base `b1d04ae5e684aa9c6807af655089efa1df8a7ed6` until those gates pass.

Card 135 remains postponed, not abandoned or accepted. Other unfinished owner/monitor/deferred obligations remain explicit; no automatic study, monitor, signing, archive, TestFlight, submission, external fact or release action is introduced. This policy reduces development waiting and repeated bookkeeping, not retained features, migration safety, test meaning or final evidence.

The new snapshot path reaches the existing canonicalPartsStockJSON converter, which attempts an Any-to-Bool bridge before inspecting NSNumber's CFBoolean type. Apple NSNumber documentation distinguishes numeric CFNumber from CFBoolean, and Swift SE-0170 defines numeric casts as value conversions rather than preservation of original boxed type; numeric zero/one therefore cannot be classified by whether a Bool cast succeeds. Within the already owned encoder, move Boolean classification behind the existing NSNumber/CFBoolean identity test so genuine JSON integers remain integers and genuine booleans remain booleans.
